## `wx/clipbrd.h`

[Upstream source](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/clipbrd.h)

0 implemented; 12 partial; 4 pending; 0 context entries. Native gaps stay visible; feature priority is separate.

- [~] [L12](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/clipbrd.h#L12) `#define wxTheClipboard` — **Partial**: `clipboard-text`, `clipboard-text-available-p`, `flush-clipboard`; ABI: `wxl_clipboard_text`, `wxl_clipboard_text_available`, `wxl_flush_clipboard`. Standard singleton clipboard text only; scoped internal Open/Close and PRIMARY restoration, no public clipboard/data-object wrapper or raw ownership. - [~] [L78](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/modalhook.h#L78) `class wxModalDialogHook {` — **Partial**: `show-modal`, `end-modal`; ABI: `wxl_dialog_show`, `wxl_dialog_end`, `wxl_dialog_abort`, `wxl_dialog_depth`. Prerequisite native workflow hook only: prompt substitution/depth/context/cancellation. No public hook class/factory, arbitrary replacement or Lisp virtual callbacks. Managed modal-only text entry with fixed defaults, native stored value and CLOS lifetime/rollback. Password/styles/validators/custom buttons and live internal text control unbound. Platform-native message/file backends remain deferred after macOS cancellation probe hang; explicit generic message/file GUI backends are supported within their documented scope.

Optional managed parent, Unicode NUL-free message/title/value, fixed native default style/position. Modal-only, direct CLOS subclasses/initargs/AFTER and rollback; no password/text-validator/style overload. Actual native parent is normalized to the explicit requested window (including hidden) or truly parentless NIL; native autoparenting is not retained.
